  uid=5_40 heading "Enable benchmarking" level="2"
  uid=5_41 StaticText "Sets all features to a fixed state; that is, disables randomization for feature states. If '(Default Feature States)' is selected, sets all features to their default state. If '(Match Field Trial Testing Config)' is selected, sets all features to the state configured in the field trial testing config. This is used by developers and testers to diagnose whether an observed problem is caused by a non-default base::Feature configuration. This flag is automatically reset after 3 restarts and will be off from the 4th restart. On the 3rd restart, the flag will appear to be off but the effect is still active."
